## 3.1 Jack Costigan (POV; the player)


**[SRC bible.md · §6 Jack — identity / public face / convictions / voice / look+phone · L243–246]**

**Public face.** The funniest guy on the floor. Charming, quick, a little dangerous, generous with his last twenty,
devastating at 2 a.m. arguments, holds a door, holds a grudge, holds his liquor less well than he thinks. The guy who
goes to Mass hungover and the guy who goes. Smokes when stressed or drinking; is quitting. Reads constantly; the
paperback in his back pocket has margins full of fights with the author.


**[SRC bible.md · §6 Jack — identity / public face / convictions / voice / look+phone · L259–263]**

**Convictions (brief).** Law-and-order Rust Belt conservative (§4.6). **Loves Benedict XVI** — the theologian-pope,
Regensburg, *Spe Salvi*, *Caritas in Veritate*, the liturgy; carries *Spe Salvi* with a Benedict holy card (S1E02); the
resignation (Feb 11, 2013) lands hard. **Struggles with Francis** (says, repeatedly, that Francis has held the line on
doctrine; hates how he treats the conservative cardinals Jack admires). **Adores Leo XIV** (2025). Gets deep into **Bishop
Barron / Word on Fire** from the *Catholicism* series (2011) and later defends him as doing the best he can.


**[SRC bible.md · §6 Jack — identity / public face / convictions / voice / look+phone · L265–273]**

**Voice.** Wry, allusive, Rust Belt blunt. Says "pop," "gumband" (rubber band; Youngstown), "jagoff" (rarely, with
affection), "yinz" never (that's Pittsburgh; he'll correct you). Quotes Chesterton, Springsteen, the Baltimore Catechism,
and his nonna, sometimes in one sentence. Jokes as deflection — when someone gets close, he gets funnier. Under real
pressure, he gets very quiet and very plain. Never says "I'm fine"; says "I'm good," which is worse.
- *"I'm not quitting. I'm tapering. Like a saint, but with Marlboros."*
- *"In Youngstown we don't say 'I love you.' We say 'Text me when you get there,' and then we don't sleep."*
- *"Chesterton says the Church is a house with a hundred gates. I came in through the one with the smoking section."*
- *"I believe in the resurrection of the body. I just don't trust mine."*
- Quiet register: *"Okay. Okay. Tell me what you need."*


**[SRC bible.md · §6 Jack — identity / public face / convictions / voice / look+phone · L280–280]**

**Phone:** a Motorola RAZR V3 with a cracked hinge, Gina's hand-me-down; T9; he texts like a telegram.


## 3.2 Lena Broussard (calls, texts, in person s18, pictures s05)


**[SRC bible.md · §7 Lena — identity / public face / convictions / voice / look+phone · L328–331]**

**Public face.** Incandescent. The girl who wins the argument, the room, the parking dispute. Funny, fast, theatrical,
generous, impossible to out-talk. Devout without apology: daily Mass when she can manage it, mantilla sometimes, a rosary
in her boot. Loves a party and loves leaving one early. Cooks for the whole floor on Sundays (red beans, a gumbo when she
can get okra). Has a boyfriend back home — everyone on the floor knows by the end of week one, because she says so.


**[SRC bible.md · §7 Lena — identity / public face / convictions / voice / look+phone · L342–345]**

**Convictions (brief).** Catholic Worker, Dorothy Day, pro-life Democrat (§4.6). **Respects Benedict XVI's mind and office;
not enthralled** ("I'd follow him into a burning building. I wouldn't put him on my wall"). **Adores Francis** — the
peripheries, mercy, the poor, *Laudato Si'*, the field hospital. **Adores Leo XIV.** Likes Barron quite a bit; grows
dissatisfied with him in the Trump years.


**[SRC bible.md · §7 Lena — identity / public face / convictions / voice / look+phone · L350–361]**

**Voice.** Fast, Southern, combative, theatrical, then suddenly — without warning — tender and plain. Long sentences that
build to a verdict. Uses "cher" (to Laney and to children), "y'all," "making groceries," "neutral ground," "lagniappe,"
"Lord have mercy" (sincerely and sarcastically). Swears creatively and Catholic-ly: "Sweet suffering Saint Sebastian."
Cites Aquinas like a lawyer cites precedent. Narrates her own outrage in the third person when very angry ("Oh, Magdalena
is *going* to say something"). When hurt she goes formal and polite, which is terrifying.
- *"I'm not against fun. I'm against fun that wants your car keys and your name."*
- *"You're carrying a New Orleans novel to California. That's either a pickup line or a cry for help, and I need to know
  which before I decide how to be disappointed."*
- *"Aquinas says grace perfects nature. He doesn't say it replaces it. You keep trying to skip to the part where God
  does your homework."*
- *"My grandmother's dead and she still makes me wear it."*
- Plain register: *"Don't. Don't be funny right now. Just stay."*


**[SRC bible.md · §7 Lena — identity / public face / convictions / voice / look+phone · L367–367]**

**Phone:** LG enV2 with the keyboard; texts in complete sentences with punctuation, which Jack finds unbearable and hot.


## 3.3 Skye Callender (texts, calls, in person, pictures) — ALL FOUR of her every scene (sex appeal · intellect · wit/play · fun)


**[SRC bible.md · §10.2 Skye — world / look+phone+car / her pull on Jack / faith / Theo / VOICE / the friends / the Costigans on Skye · L1232–1241]**

**World.** A **working actress**, already working: since 2007 a **recurring role on a fictional CW-style prep-school teen soap, *Sweetwater***
(Sweetwater Academy, a boarding school on a lake; a fictional youth-network netlet, Thursdays at nine; produced by **Meridian Television**,
Richard Vance's company — which is how she and Theo grew up on the same sets and at the same wrap parties — but Meridian's name is in the
end credits, never on her Facebook), as **Cassidy**, **the sweet, virginal good girl everyone protects** — the opposite of who she is (brief,
binding; corrected after S1E14 from "the neighbor girl the writers keep getting into trouble"). She finds it hilarious that the Costigans will
know her as "the nice one." Before that, from eleven:
commercials (a fast-food campaign people still quote at her), a season as somebody's little sister on a kids'-network sitcom. Famous
enough to be recognized at the Anchor by a college couple splitting fries; not famous enough to be left alone by anyone. For the spring
of 2010 her character is written "away at boarding school," and she enrolls at Marisol **to be normal** (undeclared: "I've declared
enough"), renting a room in **Sol Holm's** falling-down house on Del Playa (two surfers, a darkroom, a porch that leans).


**[SRC bible.md · §10.2 Skye — world / look+phone+car / her pull on Jack / faith / Theo / VOICE / the friends / the Costigans on Skye · L1246–1249]**

**Look (brief).** **Sun-gold LA**: long sun-streaked hair (blonder than Lena's honey-brown, salt in it), a tan, freckles across the nose
she has stopped covering since she left the show's makeup trailer, cutoffs and a man's flannel, bare feet whenever she can get away
with it, a braided anklet. About five-seven, rangy, a surfer's shoulders. California made flesh, and nothing like Lena — which is
exactly why Laney sees a copy. **Phone:** a white iPhone 3GS. **Car:** a dented 1991 Saab 900 convertible her mother hates.


**[SRC bible.md · §10.2 Skye — world / look+phone+car / her pull on Jack / faith / Theo / VOICE / the friends / the Costigans on Skye · L1250–1254]**

**Her pull on Jack (brief).** **She dares him** — the reckless Costigan comes alive around her (the harbor ladder at 1 a.m., the Point
break at dawn, a drive to LA on a school night). **She out-argues him** — not philosophy; *people*: she reads a room the way he reads a
book, and she is usually right about who wants what. **She sees him** — and it means a great deal, but it isn't Lena's kind of seeing:
Lena shares the faith, so she almost always knows *what* she's looking at; Skye sees Jack clearly and, missing the one fundamental thing,
**doesn't always interpret it right** (she reads his restraint as fear, his guilt as Catholic decor, his prayer as a breathing exercise).


**[SRC bible.md · §10.2 Skye — world / look+phone+car / her pull on Jack / faith / Theo / VOICE / the friends / the Costigans on Skye · L1255–1258]**

**Faith.** She can follow his prayer life to a point — she likens it to yoga and meditation, sincerely ("You sit still and listen for
something. I do that. I just don't expect anybody to answer.") — and she can follow his God as philosophy, which genuinely attracts her
(the First Mover as a plot problem; the Incarnation as "the best twist anyone ever wrote"). **She never truly gets it.** The logic on paper,
never the thing itself. That is why they never click at the deepest level, and the show never makes her stupid for it.


**[SRC bible.md · §10.2 Skye — world / look+phone+car / her pull on Jack / faith / Theo / VOICE / the friends / the Costigans on Skye · L1265–1271]**

**Theo.** **Childhood friends** — the same sets, the same parties, practically siblings who have seen each other at their worst (a wrap
party at fourteen — her first drink, his first time holding somebody's hair back in a production trailer — that she won't discuss because she
was sick on his shoes; a night at seventeen he doesn't discuss, which is his and stays 🔒). *(Standards guard, Head Writer after S1E14: the
fourteen-year-old wrap party is **non-sexual by canon** — embarrassment, not harm; never imply abuse, never use it as texture or a twist.)* He knows exactly what she is, loves her anyway, and stays **Team
Lena**: perfectly polite to her face (alliance rules), feeding Laney intel. She calls him **"Theodore Ashby"** in a butler's voice when she's
performing and **"Ted"** when she isn't (nobody else does; Richard's "Teddy" stays Richard's). Theo on her, short, true register: *"She's
the best actress I know, Costigan. Never ask her if she's acting."*


**[SRC bible.md · §10.2 Skye — world / look+phone+car / her pull on Jack / faith / Theo / VOICE / the friends / the Costigans on Skye · L1279–1286]**

**Voice (brief): playful and performative.** Always a little "on": voices, bits, characters, banter like a scene partner (she'll play his
mother, his confessor, a Youngstown waitress, Hale, the Pope); she answers questions as somebody else first. You never quite know when she's
being real, and **when she drops it, it's devastating** — short, flat, no music. Her humor is **a professional's improv** (status games,
yes-and, a callback three scenes later) — distinct from **Laney's** Southern pageant chaos and from **Theo's** lying-by-over-specifying. She
never uses Laney's "sugar" or Theo's loglines.
- *"Yes, and — your God is the Father, the Son and a bird. I've worked with worse ensembles."*
- *"You put* now *on it. Now is the most specific date there is."* / *"Visual aids."* (S1E21 — the argument; supersedes the struck "Say no again. Slower.")
- Real register: *"You're the only person who's ever told me no and meant it. Do you know what that does to a person?"*


**[SRC bible.md · §10.2 Skye — world / look+phone+car / her pull on Jack / faith / Theo / VOICE / the friends / the Costigans on Skye · L1287–1289]**

**The friends (brief).** **Laney** is openly rude to her, like all the others — and privately, begrudgingly likes her (cigarettes on the Pi Lam
roof; never admitted; Laney's only Jack-girl friendship). **Lena can't stand her** — and does not tell Laney to be nice. **Theo** polite, Team Lena.
**Margot** finds her "the only interesting person Costigan has ever dated, which is an indictment."


**[SRC bible.md · §10.2 Skye — world / look+phone+car / her pull on Jack / faith / Theo / VOICE / the friends / the Costigans on Skye · L1304–1309]**

**The Costigans on Skye (brief).** They never meet her (no chance between January and New Orleans); they find her Facebook over
Christmas (S2E01–E02; moved up from S2E05 by the acceleration). When they find out — **especially once they see her
Facebook and who she is** (the show, the photos; **Gina** has known since Wed Nov 25 — she looked Skye up for Jack on the phone from Denver and screamed, E17 rev. 4 — and kept it; at Christmas the family finds the page on the family Dell, Gina's secret or Gina's doing, room's call) — they are **all the way out**: **Nonna**
by rosary and dark Calabrese; **Gina** mortified and thrilled about the famous part; **Maria and Frank** worried about "that kind of girl," and all of
them asking about Lena. **Except Danny**, who thinks it's hilarious and that Jack must have tricked her somehow (he has no Facebook: Gina describes the
photos in detail on the Wednesday call; his letter: "IS SHE BLIND OR DID YOU LIE"). S2E01–E03.


## 3.4 The Costigans (Fr. Dom Bruno; Frank; Maria; Danny; Gina; Rosie; Nonna) — §10.8, abridged to the characterization and the S2 plan paragraphs  [contains 🔒 Danny/Struthers material: it constrains, it is never surfaced]


**[SRC bible.md · §10.8 JACK'S FAMILY — the Costigans (home, Fr. Dom, Frank, Maria, Danny + his letter voice, Gina, Rosie, Nonna, the S2E02/S2E03 plan paragraphs). [Packer: the E06/E10–E14 plan histories omitted] · L1699–1722]**

\### 10.8 JACK'S FAMILY — the Costigans of Youngstown
Home: a narrow two-story frame house on **Glenwood Avenue near Brownlee Woods**, South Side, with a detached garage where
Frank keeps a space heater, a radio and a Stroh's fridge. Parish: **St. Brigid of Kildare** (fictional), South Side. Pastor:
**Fr. Dom Bruno** (named E06 plan; sixties, skipped the homily on Ephesians 5 the Sunday before Jack left — Aug 23, 2009).

- **Francis "Frank" Costigan, 51.** GM Lordstown assembly, 26 years, UAW Local 1112. Laid off since the GM bankruptcy
  (June 1, 2009); unemployment and the last of the SUB pay are running out by October. Drives a **1999 Chevy Silverado**
  (forest green, rust over the rear wheel wells, 212,000 miles). Sings Sinatra in the garage. Kennedy Democrat, pro-life,
  union to the marrow. Funny in a gruff, minimal way. Loves his kids ferociously and can't say it. 🔒 **Frank knows Jack
  threw the punch.** He brokered the plea: he went to Danny in the county jail and Danny said "Jackie's got the
  scholarship, Dad," and Frank let him. He has chosen one son's future over the other son's freedom, and he has to look at
  both of them. He drove Jack 2,400 miles because he couldn't afford two plane tickets and because he wanted to see the
  Pacific once before he died. He sleeps in the truck on the way home to save the motel money.
  - *"Close your mouth. You'll catch flies."* · *"Don't waste it."* · *"We don't. Not here. That's done."*
- **Maria Teresa (Rinaldi) Costigan, 49.** Secretary at St. Brigid's school. Devout, fierce, funny, tired. Visits Danny at
  Trumbull Correctional every other Sunday after the 8 a.m. Mass. 🔒 **Does not know** the truth. Believes Danny's version
  (a fight that went wrong). Her grief over Danny is the family's weather.
  - *"Did you eat? Don't lie to me, I can hear it in your voice when you haven't eaten."*
- **Daniel Francis "Danny" Costigan, 22** (b. Feb 2, 1987). 🔒 Serving five years at **Trumbull Correctional Institution**
  (Leavittsburg, Ohio) for felonious assault, pleaded January 2008. Eligible for judicial release consideration in 2011;
  release date 2012. He took the fall for Jack. He is funny, profane, gentle, does push-ups and reads every book Jack sends
  him (Jack sends one a month, used, with his own margin notes erased because the prison won't allow writing — Jack writes
  new notes on a separate sheet). In his letters he calls Jack "Professor." He does not resent Jack. That's the worst
  part for Jack.


**[SRC bible.md · §10.8 JACK'S FAMILY — the Costigans (home, Fr. Dom, Frank, Maria, Danny + his letter voice, Gina, Rosie, Nonna, the S2E02/S2E03 plan paragraphs). [Packer: the E06/E10–E14 plan histories omitted] · L1731–1739]**

  **(E08 plan:) Danny's second letter** — his answer to Jack's Sept 21 letter (which gave him Lena's name), in the Delgado box
  **Tue Sep 29, Michaelmas**: opens "Happy St. Mike's, Professor" (🔒 Danny was his confirmation sponsor; never stated); Reggie
  has decided the Pacific smells like "money and fish, which is California"; *Brideshead* is still with the C.O. ("he says it's
  about a house"); Gina told him on the Wednesday call that "the veil girl" has a boyfriend — "Gina says a lot of things. Tell
  Lena hey anyway. Lena. I like knowing it." The sentence that stops Jack cold: **"You sound happy out there. Don't feel bad about
  it. Somebody in this family ought to be. That's an order."** (🔒 the man carrying Jack's cross absolving him in advance, in the
  week Jack is guiltiest about something else; never glossed.) The Hopkins (margins erased — *Show Lena* erased by Lena herself,
  in the Delgado laundry room, Wed night) and Hale's Housman ship to Trumbull through Driftwood Thu Oct 1; Jack's reply says the
  Housman is "from a man who doesn't sleep" (`e7_housman=="sleep"`).


**[SRC bible.md · §10.8 JACK'S FAMILY — the Costigans (home, Fr. Dom, Frank, Maria, Danny + his letter voice, Gina, Rosie, Nonna, the S2E02/S2E03 plan paragraphs). [Packer: the E06/E10–E14 plan histories omitted] · L1744–1753]**

- **Regina "Gina" Costigan, 15** (b. Apr 9, 1994). Sophomore at Ursuline High School. **(E05 canon:)** in sophomore
  religion, during the unit on the corporal works of mercy ("visit the imprisoned"), **Ashley Vrabel** says Gina's the class
  expert on the imprisoned; the room laughs; the teacher says "Ladies" and moves on. Ashley's father got a DUI last
  Christmas (South Side gossip; nobody was hurt — 🔒 the lucky driver). Smart-mouthed, Facebook-native,
  texts Jack constantly in lowercase. Bullied at school as "the convict's sister" (S1 subplot). Jack's favorite person to
  argue with. **(E07 plan:)** knowing the nun's name since E06, she friend-requests "Magdalena Broussard" on Facebook; Lena
  accepts and writes on Gina's wall (signed —L.); Gina finds Lena's "In a Relationship with Wesley Arceneaux" and Becca's
  two-exclamation-point post on Jack's wall, and says the obvious in lowercase. She does not tell Nonna. **Maria (E07, the
  Saturday call):** learns "Idaho" from Gina (Gina showed her Becca's profile picture) and is delighted ("Seven!"); then asks
  after the veil girl.


**[SRC bible.md · §10.8 JACK'S FAMILY — the Costigans (home, Fr. Dom, Frank, Maria, Danny + his letter voice, Gina, Rosie, Nonna, the S2E02/S2E03 plan paragraphs). [Packer: the E06/E10–E14 plan histories omitted] · L1754–1755]**

  **(E18 plan, rev. 2:)** Gina is the family's translator for Nonna's Calabrese (Jack's Italian is church-and-kitchen); sworn on Skye since Denver (E17), she takes Danny's Wednesday call and holds ("I said I have mono"),
  translates Nonna's interrogation of Theo at the Thanksgiving table, and nearly blurts "Cass—" in front of Theo (path). **New canon (E18 plan): the Costigan rule — the guest says grace** (Frank's; Theo says it at Thanksgiving 2009).


**[SRC bible.md · §10.8 JACK'S FAMILY — the Costigans (home, Fr. Dom, Frank, Maria, Danny + his letter voice, Gina, Rosie, Nonna, the S2E02/S2E03 plan paragraphs). [Packer: the E06/E10–E14 plan histories omitted] · L1756–1768]**

- **Rosaria "Rosie" Costigan, 7** (b. Oct 7, 2001 — Our Lady of the Rosary). Makes Jack drawings and mails them. Thinks
  California is made of beaches and Hannah Montana.
- **Nonna — Carmela (Ferraro) Rinaldi, 84.** Born in San Giovanni in Fiore, Calabria; came over 1951. Widow of Nonno
  Antonio Rinaldi, a Youngstown Sheet & Tube man who lost his job on **Black Monday, September 19, 1977**, and never
  recovered. Lives with the Costigans. Prays the rosary for Jack by name. Slips him twenties. Calls him "Giacomino."
  Early dementia beginning in S1 — **first on the page in E06** (Sat Sep 19, the phone): she asks *"E la ragazza?"* about
  the girl, gets her answer, and five minutes later asks it again in the same words, as new; Maria takes the phone back:
  "She's tired, honey." No gloss; Jack notices and tells only Lena (two-hander). 🔒 **Nonna knows.** She saw Jack's hand the morning after — the split knuckles, the
  stitched eyebrow, the blood under his nails — and said nothing. In S1E18 (Thanksgiving morning, St. Elizabeth's, the hour of her discharge — untranslated on the page) she says to him, in Italian, *"Tuo fratello
  porta la tua croce"* — your brother is carrying your cross. 🔒 Nonna dies in S3 (Lent 2011? or Advent 2010 — decide by
  path); Lena comes to the funeral in Youngstown.
  **(E08 plan:)** Maria told her the veil girl has a boyfriend (`e7_veil=="boyfriend"`): "Peccato." Then, sharp as a tack about
  the one thing: **"E l'altra?"** — and the other one? (Idaho.) Her memory for the Church calendar is perfect while the rest


**[SRC bible.md · §10.8 JACK'S FAMILY — the Costigans (home, Fr. Dom, Frank, Maria, Danny + his letter voice, Gina, Rosie, Nonna, the S2E02/S2E03 plan paragraphs). [Packer: the E06/E10–E14 plan histories omitted] · L1807–1809]**

  **(S2E02 plan:)** 🔒 **Frank has never been inside Trumbull.** Since Danny's transfer (early 2008) he has driven Maria every other Sunday and waited in the far end of the lot with the radio (S1E18, S2E01 — "no reason given" on the page). The reason is §10.8's first bullet: he brokered the plea, and he cannot sit across a table from the son who took it with the son it was taken for beside him. **On Holy Family Sunday, Dec 27, 2009, he goes in for the first time** ("Where do I stand?" — Maria: "You sit, Frank. You sit with him."); Danny stands up when he sees him; they talk about the Browns and nothing else; Frank brings Danny coffee with cream and two sugars, the way Danny took it at fifteen — Danny drinks it black now, and drinks it ("It's good, Pop."). The page never says why he never came, and never reads him. From here Frank is on the visiting list in earnest (the E03+ planners decide how often he goes back). ~~🔒 Danny's private sentence, Dec 27: "Don't leave nobody in the truck, Professor."~~ **[SUPERSEDED by the S2E02 plan rev. 2 — read as advice tipping the double state, against the brief's "one hard question… no advice."]** **Danny's private line, Dec 27** (while Maria teaches Frank the vending machines) is **a plain request about their father**: "Look at him." … **"Third's Ma's Sunday. You're still home." "Till the ninth." "Don't let him sit in the truck."** — not about either girl; it may be a `key`. Dec 20's one question ("Which one of 'em can you tell the truth to?") stays Danny's only word on the girls, never repeated or glossed; it pays in New Orleans and at S2E30. **S2E03 (Sun Jan 3, Maria's Sunday)** may say in one line whether Frank goes in again. Frank is on Danny's list by name ("Costigan, Francis."). Maria to Danny at the table: "You knew before me." / "I know everything, Ma. I got time." ("He misses the beach" is retired after S2E01.) **Gina writes Danny about the yacht** (Tue Dec 29: "How do you spell *yacht*?") → his letter IS SHE BLIND OR DID YOU LIE (S2E03). **Nonna renames Skye "Cassidy"** (the marathon, Dec 26) and prays for her by it ("*per Cassidy*"). **Rosie's midnight** (new canon, a Costigan custom since Gina was small): on New Year's Eve the family does midnight at nine for the youngest — a taped ball drop, then pots and pans on the porch. **The Benedettos** (Carly's family) are St. Brigid's people and live two streets from Glenwood. **Maria goes to the seven o'clock Mass on December 31st every year** (S2E02 rev. 2; Jack goes with her in 2009).
  **(S2E03 plan rev. 1:)** **Nonna's New Year's lentils** (one spoon each "*per i soldi*"; Frank takes two). **Jan 2: Lena calls the Glenwood landline on purpose (she got the number from information) and Maria answers — their first conversation** (the baby, the coffee — "*That* was you" — the book; "She wants you. I don't know why."); Nonna, from the chair, only "*La suora?*". **Jan 3, Maria's Sunday: Frank turns the engine off before anybody asks** and goes in a second time; "They'll win." (they do — four straight). **Jan 5–6: La Befana** — Rosie's stocking; Jack's **sugar coal** (*carbone dolce*) in one of Danny's old socks ("*Dolce.*" / "*Un poco.*"); the Magi moved a little each day to the crib. **Jan 6: the lights come down, and 🔒 the Stroh's fridge in the garage is stocked with Canada Dry** — Frank's "after" ([`s2e1_frank_xmas`] "This is after."); never glossed, never a vow on the page; whether it holds is S3's, and a relapse is never played as a twist. **Jan 7: Danny's letter, IS SHE BLIND OR DID YOU LIE** (Gina's *yacht*; "Pop came twice. Tell him the coffee's fine black."; "Write me back the truth. Not the boat."). **Jan 9: Nonna's twenty and "*Prego per tutt'e due*"; Maria returns the empty Café du Monde can for "your friend"; Gina: "Don't let Ma find out from Facebook."; Frank at PIT: "Call your mother when you land. She don't sleep."** **Jan 10, 10:37 p.m.: Jack calls Maria from Buellton before his name goes on the status** — "Is she Catholic *yet*?" / "Thank you for calling me first." / **"Does Lena know?"**


## 3.5 The Broussards (Tony, Celeste, Luc, Tess, Remy, Jude/Josie, Mémère, Lucille) — §10.9, abridged  [contains 🔒 Memorial/Celeste material; the S2E03 paragraph is last]


**[SRC bible.md · §10.9 LENA'S FAMILY — the Broussards (home; Tony; Celeste; Luc; Tess; Remy; Jude/Josie; Mémère; the S2E03 plan paragraph). [Packer: the E11/E14 plan bullets omitted] · L1810–1843]**

\### 10.9 LENA'S FAMILY — the Broussards of New Orleans
Home: a raised double shotgun converted to a single on **N. Dupre Street, Mid-City**, near Bayou St. John. Took 4½ feet
of water in 2005; rebuilt by Tony and his brothers by hand over two years; the waterline is still visible on the shed.
Parish: **Our Lady of the Holy Rosary** (Esplanade Ave.) — the family pews; Lena's First Communion; the Christmas Eve
crawl. Family business: **Broussard's Seafood**, a market on Banks Street.

- **Antoine "Tony" Broussard, 53.** Owns Broussard's Seafood. Loud, sentimental, a world-class griller and grudge-holder,
  Saints season-ticket holder since 1981 (through the Aints years, with a bag on his head in 1980 — family lore). Cajun
  roots in Breaux Bridge; talks to God like a business partner who owes him money.
  - *"Cher, you call your mama or I'm flying out there and I'm bringing the boudin."*
- **Celeste (Guidry) Broussard, 50.** Registered nurse; in 2005 an ICU nurse at **Memorial Medical Center** (Uptown);
  now at Touro Infirmary. Brisk, funny, ferociously competent, gives orders in a whisper. 🔒 **Her mother, Lucille Guidry
  (Lena's grandmother, "Grandmère"), was a long-term acute care patient on the hospital's seventh floor during Katrina, and
  was among those who died there before evacuation.** Celeste was working in the ICU downstairs. On the fourth day she was
  ordered onto a boat with other staff, and went, believing her mother would be evacuated within hours. She was not.
  Celeste has never told her family what she knew or didn't, what she asked or didn't ask, what she was told. The
  coroner returned Lucille's Miraculous Medal in a plastic bag in February 2006; Celeste gave it to Lena at fifteen
  without a word. Lena has never asked. Sheri Fink's long investigation of the Memorial deaths was published in *The New
  York Times Magazine* in late August 2009 — **the week of the pilot.** Celeste texts Lena: *"Don't read the Times this
  week, cher."* 🔒 *Writers' rule:* the show never asserts what any real person at Memorial did or did not do. The
  Broussards' grief is about **uncertainty, absence and a daughter's choice**. Celeste's own culpability, if any, is the
  choice to leave on the boat. The truth Celeste finally tells (adult seasons): she went back up the stairs before she
  left, and her mother was asleep, and she kissed her and left her, and she has wondered every day since if her mother
  ever woke.
- **Luc Broussard, 24.** Eldest; NOPD officer (joined 2006). Wes's best friend. Quiet, good, under pressure: in 2010 the
  federal Danziger Bridge indictments and other post-Katrina NOPD scandals tear at his department. 🔒 He knew things
  about his precinct's conduct in the storm's aftermath that he never reported — a mirror for Lena (S2–S3).
- **Thérèse "Tess" (Broussard) Fontenot, 22.** Married at 21 to Beau Fontenot, a lineman for Entergy; first baby due
  December 2009 (a girl, **Lucille**, born Dec 19, 2009 — Lena becomes godmother over Christmas break).
- **Remy Broussard, 20.** At LSU; party boy; Tiger Stadium; adores Lena; the family's comic disaster.
- **Jude and Josie Broussard, 13.** Twins. Wes carried them out of a neighbor's second floor in 2005. Jude is quiet; Josie
  is Lena in miniature.
- **Mémère — Odile Broussard, 88.** Tony's mother, Breaux Bridge, speaks Cajun French first, makes the best gumbo in
  Louisiana, thinks Jack is "un bon garçon mais trop maigre."


**[SRC bible.md · §10.9 LENA'S FAMILY — the Broussards (home; Tony; Celeste; Luc; Tess; Remy; Jude/Josie; Mémère; the S2E03 plan paragraph). [Packer: the E11/E14 plan bullets omitted] · L1857–1858]**

- **(S2E03 plan rev. 1:)** **Lucille Odile is baptized Sun Jan 3, 2010, at Our Lady of the Holy Rosary — in the third quarter of Carolina–New Orleans** (Panthers 23–10, starters rested), as she was born in one; Lena godmother; Tony with a radio in his coat. **The christening gown is Grandmère Lucille's sewing** — made for Luc in 1985, worn by all five, kept in **Mémère's cedar chest in Breaux Bridge**, which is why it survived the storm (the only thing Grandmère made still in the family; Lena ironed it herself). 🔒 **Wes came to the house after, not the church** (Luc asked him to stand in the back; his reason is the one he gave Lena on Nov 2 — never on the page). **Julian learned the Creed for it and didn't say it; held Lena's purse; flew home to Oakland Jan 4 with Mémère's pralines and a holy card** ("come back"); **Mémère calls him *le petit violon*** (Tess's album caption). **Twelfth Night (Jan 6): Lena got the baby in the first king cake** — so the next one is hers; **she flies back Sun Jan 10 with a king cake on her lap**; Laney meets her at SBA at four (Kevin drives). Lucille grabbed Lena's medal at the goodbye and Tess had to open her fingers one at a time. 🔒 That night, in 217, Lena sees the status (§23.1 S2E03).


## 3.6 Theo Vance


**[SRC bible.md · §8.1 Theo — public face / voice · L489–492]**

**Public face.** Golden LA prince. Gorgeous, silver-tongued, exhausting, generous to the point of insult. Pays for
everything. Knows every bouncer. Makes everyone feel like the most interesting person at the party for ninety seconds.
Constantly tempting Jack: the drink, the girl, the drive to LA at midnight, the shortcut. Loves Jack immediately and
completely — this is not a performance, and it's the only thing about Theo that isn't.


**[SRC bible.md · §8.1 Theo — public face / voice · L508–517]**

**Voice.** Epigrammatic, luxurious, cinematic. Speaks in loglines and pitches ("Picture it: a Catholic, a keg, a
cliffside — "). Movie references constantly (Kubrick, Altman, Truffaut, *Point Break* unironically). Calls everyone by
their last name or an invented title ("Costigan," "Father Jack," "Professor," "my moral compass," "Pruitt the Destroyer").
Never says "sorry"; says "I owe you a kidney." When he's lying he gets *more* specific. When he's telling the truth he
gets short.
- *"I'm not a bad influence, Costigan. I'm an influence. The bad is your contribution."*
- *"Pray for me. Preemptively. Like a flu shot."*
- *"My father doesn't have feelings. He has people who have them for him."*
- *"Don't look at me like that. You look like a stained-glass window that's disappointed in me."*
- True register: *"Don't. Okay? Just — not there."*


## 3.7 Laney Price


**[SRC bible.md · §8.2 Laney — public face / voice · L560–563]**

**Public face.** Redhead bombshell, laugh you can hear across a party, former pageant girl (Miss Teen Dallas County 2007
first runner-up), sorority rusher at the top house (Pi Lambda Chi). The wild one. The one who dragged Lena to the party.
Has a Xanax prescription and a doctor's note "for flying" and takes it for everything else. Fiercely loyal: will fight a
man twice her size for a girl she met ten minutes ago.


**[SRC bible.md · §8.2 Laney — public face / voice · L575–582]**

**Voice.** Dallas pageant-Texan, filthy timing, malapropisms she makes on purpose, sugar-coated threats. "Sugar," "y'all,"
"fixin' to," "bless your heart" (weaponized), "I will end you, and I will look fabulous at your funeral." Talks in
superlatives. Narrates her own life like a pageant host ("And our next contestant is a hangover").
- *"Doctor's orders. Literally. I have a note. It's laminated."*
- *"Magdalena Broussard, the Jell-O shots are sentient and they're asking for you."*
- *"You look like a sad Irish poem. Dance with me or I'm calling your mother."*
- *"I'm not a mess, sugar. I'm a *destination*."*
- True register (rare, usually at 4 a.m.): *"Don't leave. I'm not drunk. I mean I am. But don't."*


## 3.8 Margot Lindqvist


**[SRC bible.md · §8.3 Margot — public face / voice · L661–665]**

**Public face.** Militant feminist atheist. Clove cigarettes (Djarum Black — the FDA flavored-cigarette ban takes effect
**September 22, 2009** and she holds a wake). Writes for *The Marisol Tide*. Keeps a running score on the Delgado lounge
whiteboard: "USEFUL IDIOTS FOR CHRISTIAN NATIONALISM." Regularly accuses Lena of internalized misogyny, Jack of upholding
the patriarchy, and both of being useful idiots for Christian nationalism. Becomes, for reasons none of them can explain,
one of their closest friends for life.


**[SRC bible.md · §8.3 Margot — public face / voice · L676–684]**

**Voice.** Deadpan, polysyllabic, merciless, footnoted. Uses academic jargon on purpose and knows how it sounds
("heteronormative," "problematize," "hegemonic," "praxis" — she's allowed 2009 jargon and is mocked for it). Never
raises her voice. Insults as diagnoses. Humor bone-dry and frequently cruel; when she's kind she pretends it's research.
- *"St. Michael. Patron saint of what, bouncers?"*
- *"You're a Catholic at a kegger. Which one of us is the anthropologist?"*
- *"I'm not cold. I'm Scandinavian. It's a different thing."*
- *"You're going to be one of those Catholics who's fun. Those are the dangerous ones."*
- *"Lena, I say this with love: you've internalized a two-thousand-year-old men's club and you call it a personality."*
- True register: *"Can you just — sit here. Don't talk. I'll know if you're praying."*


## 3.9 Kevin Tran


**[SRC bible.md · §8.4 Kevin — public face / voice · L739–741]**

**Public face.** Gym-built pre-med, the nicest guy in any room, Newman stalwart, the one who knows everybody's name and
birthday and remembers your mom's surgery. Altar server, lector, intramural flag football. Dad jokes. Iron-clad public
persona.


**[SRC bible.md · §8.4 Kevin — public face / voice · L755–762]**

**Voice.** Sunny, dad-joke, team-captain. Bay Area "hella" when relaxed (rarely). Organizes things. Medical vocabulary
as comedy ("That's a textbook case of acute Theo"). Under the sunshine a scalpel: the occasional line so precise it
draws blood, followed instantly by a smile. Code-switches to Vietnamese with family; his Vietnamese is worse than he
pretends.
- *"Two truths and a lie: I'm pre-med, I'm an only child, I've never been to a party."*
- *"Respectfully, bro, that's not a hangover, that's a cry for help with a headache."*
- *"I don't drink at these. I'm the designated adult. It's like being designated driver but for your soul."*
- True register: *"You think I don't know what people see? I built what people see. I'm good at it."*


## 3.10 Fr. Gabriel Osei


**[SRC bible.md · §8.5 Osei — public face / voice  · L808–811]**

**Public face.** Warm, unflappable, laughs from the belly, sees through everyone. A former physician. Fixes the courtyard
sprinkler himself in clerical shirt and rubber boots. Gives short homilies with one sentence you can't stop hearing.
Confessions Saturdays 3:30–4:45 "or whenever you knock — I was a doctor, I am used to being woken." Plays Ghanaian
highlife in his office loudly. Beats students at chess and lets them think it was close. A slight limp he never explains.


**[SRC bible.md · §8.5 Osei — public face / voice  · L828–836]**

**Voice.** Ghanaian English, unhurried, proverbial, mischievous; drops the occasional Twi word ("Ɛyɛ" — it is well;
"Me da wo ase" — thank you). Physician's metaphors. Asks questions instead of giving answers. Never preaches in
conversation; saves it for the pulpit and keeps it short there.
- *"I spent eight years listening to hearts with a stethoscope and six listening through a screen. The stethoscope lies
  less."*
- *"Youngstown? I know it only from the Springsteen song. Is it as sad as he says?"*
- *"That's not a hockey puck. I was a doctor. That's a ring. Or a tooth."*
- *"The Church is a hospital, my friend. Nobody is embarrassed to be sick in a hospital. Only here."*
- *"When a man jokes that much, it is because the truth is heavy and he is carrying it alone."*


## 3.11 Prof. Adrian Hale


**[SRC bible.md · §8.6 Hale — public face / voice · L889–892]**

**Public face.** Celebrity public intellectual. Hitchens' swagger, Williams' depth. The lecture course everyone takes
(PHIL 1, "God, Mind and Morals," 400 students) and the honors seminar everyone wants (PHIL 9H, "The Examined Life,"
twelve freshmen, by application). Brilliant, funny, cruel when bored, kind when no one is looking. Drinks too much,
publicly. Loves his best students, maybe too much — not sexually; possessively, paternally, hungrily.


**[SRC bible.md · §8.6 Hale — public face / voice · L910–917]**

**Voice.** Cut-glass Oxbridge with Yorkshire underneath (the vowels come out when he's drunk or angry: "bath" goes short).
Gutter vocabulary deployed with precision. Epigrams. Calls students by surname. Says "darling" to men and women equally
when drunk. Quotes Larkin, Housman, Hume and the Book of Common Prayer — the last without noticing.
- *"Prove to me you have a soul, Mr. Costigan. You have ninety minutes and I've had lunch."*
- *"Faith, Miss Broussard, is what we call it when a clever girl stops asking questions on purpose."*
- *"I don't hate God. One can't hate a fictional character. One can only hate his fans."*
- *"Come and say it to my face."*
- Drunk, true: *"Lighten our darkness, we beseech thee, O Lord — Christ, listen to me."*


## 3.12 Julian Park-Ellis (photos only; named by Tess's wall, Gina, and Lena in s09 — he never speaks)


**[SRC bible.md · §8.7 Julian — public face / unbelief / voice · L985–987]**

**Public face.** Quietly handsome; calm; the one you'd trust with anything. A **singer-songwriter** (player co-design, after S1E13):
open mics, a guitar, quiet earnest songs; sings at Café Bastille's Thursday open mic, records in his garage onto a Tascam four-track.
Secular; gentle; heartbreakingly decent. Everyone's favorite person to sit next to.


**[SRC bible.md · §8.7 Julian — public face / unbelief / voice · L989–990]**

**His unbelief (player co-design): he never had it.** Raised by kind, secular, ethical, unchurched parents; no hostility, no wound, no
lost faith — he simply never felt the need. He **finds Lena's faith beautiful, like music he can't play.**


**[SRC bible.md · §8.7 Julian — public face / unbelief / voice · L1017–1021]**

**Voice.** Soft-spoken, wry, precise; musical metaphors ("that's a suspended chord, it wants to resolve"). Asks real
questions and waits for the answer. Rarely jokes; when he does, it's sly and kind. Says "hella" once a season.
- *"I don't believe any of it. But I believe you do, and I'd like to know what that's like. Is that allowed?"*
- *"Every song I like is in a key that sounds like church. I'm told that's not an accident."*
- *"You don't have to convert me. You just have to let me stand next to you while you do the thing."*


## 3.13 Wes Arceneaux (named by Lena in s03a only)


**[SRC bible.md · §10.1 Wes — Voice · L1215–1217]**

**Voice.** Easy, slow, goofy, Gentilly-Y'at ("where y'at," "dawlin'," "yeah you right"). Talks about the Saints like
scripture. Underneath, long pauses.
- *"You're the Ohio guy. She said you were a jagoff. That means she likes you, I've known her since she had a retainer."*


## 3.14 Becca Lindell (s17 — the steps)


**[SRC bible.md · §10.4 Becca — who she is + how she tries / the friends / voice · L1472–1484]**

**Who she is.** Cute, sweet, warm, frank, funny in a dry, delighted way, much tougher than she looks — pulled a calf at
twelve, can butcher a deer, whistles a herd off a county road with no hands. Wants to be married by twenty-one and says so on
the second date; wants seven or nine children ("Odd numbers, so there's a tiebreaker"). Everyone at Newman thinks she and
Jack are perfect together; Mrs. Fitch, Tyler and the Coffee & Donuts table say "you two" by October. **A sincere, shallow
cradle Catholic:** Mass every Sunday of her life, the Glory Be when a cow is kicking her, her grandmother's rosary, Newman
like a family — and she has never wrestled with any of it. "I don't need it to be complicated. I need it to be true. It's
true. So."
**How she tries** (with his faith *and* his rigor): asks him to teach her the Five Ways and learns them by heart on a set of
bleachers, delighted and untroubled (E07); reads Aquinas with color-coded tabs from her clipboard; reads *Caritas in
Veritate* because he quoted it on a beach; comes to Hale's public lecture in her nicest cardigan; reads O'Connor and hates it
("Why is everybody so *mean*?"), reads Percy and loves it; asks Osei a question so good he laughs for a full minute (she has
no idea why it was good). Jack's arguments delight her and slide off. Asked if she ever thinks it might not be true: "No," —
honestly puzzled — "Do you?" She respects his line and does not test it; she is waiting too.


**[SRC bible.md · §10.4 Becca — who she is + how she tries / the friends / voice · L1489–1492]**

**The friends (brief).** **Laney hates her on principle** and is openly rude ("Is she Amish or just punctual?" — E07);
**Lena tells Laney to be nice — and means it, which is worse**; **Theo is flawlessly polite to her face and texts Laney her
schedule** (always Team Lena). Margot can't find anything to object to and finds that suspicious. Kevin likes her and tells
Theo and Laney to leave it alone.


**[SRC bible.md · §10.4 Becca — who she is + how she tries / the friends / voice · L1496–1499]**

**Voice.** Frank, unembarrassed, practical; ranch competence as comedy; scientific about feelings; never sarcastic; never a
joke at her faith.
- *"I've pulled a calf. Give it here."* · *"You can think about it. I'm patient. We had a whole talk."* · *"Glory Be. You can
  get through it while a cow's kicking you."*


## 3.15 Priya Raman, Evan Marsh, Caleb Hargrove, Yusuf Noorzai, Doug Pruitt, Gloria Mendes, Rudy


**[SRC bible.md · §10.5 Priya — identity+public / voice · L1615–1627]**

**Born October 14, 1988.** Fremont, California. Junior, Biochemistry. Delgado Hall 4th-floor RA (Room 401). Three jobs
(RA, library circulation, organic chem tutor). Overwhelmed; laminated everything; "per my email"; color-coded door tags;
an air horn for emergencies. Parents run a motel in Fremont and believe she's pre-med. 🔒 She's quietly failing organic
chemistry and has changed her major on paper without telling them.

🔒 **Nora connection:** Priya was Nora Calloway's freshman roommate (Delgado 2007–08, Room 222) and her best friend that
year. They drifted sophomore year (Nora moved to Serra Hall with Sloane Kittredge, got secretive). Priya has a box of Nora's
things in the RA storage closet — the Calloways never collected it — including Nora's journal, which Priya has never been
able to open past the first page. Her rule "Nobody goes to the Point after dark" is personal. On Nora's birthday she buys two mylar balloons at the Safeway
(two for one), ties one to the cross before her 8 a.m. orgo lab, in daylight, and keeps the other in the 4th-floor lounge
with a grocery cupcake. Facebook keeps suggesting Nora to her residents (no memorial status exists until late Oct 2009); she
has reported it three times. She knows the Memorare by heart because Nora prayed it in the shower every morning (Priya is
Hindu).


**[SRC bible.md · §10.5 Priya — identity+public / voice · L1629–1631]**

**Voice.** Rapid, bureaucratic, accidentally hilarious, passive-aggressive in Comic Sans. "Per the Residential Life
Handbook." "I'm going to need you to not." When she breaks, she breaks completely, then re-laminates.
- *"Rule one: nobody goes to the Point after dark. That's not a Res Life rule. That's my rule. I will know."*


**[SRC bible.md · §10.7 Evan Marsh — identity / look / voice (Hale's reader; background only) · L1663–1665]**

**Born February 11, 1980.** Duluth, Minnesota. PhD candidate in Philosophy (dissertation on Kierkegaard and moral
injury, Hale advising, year seven, stalled). TA for PHIL 1's Thursday 4 p.m. section (Hollis 108) — Jack and Lena's
section. Lapsed Lutheran. Dry, exhausted, precise, kind to struggling students, savage to lazy ones.


**[SRC bible.md · §10.7 Evan Marsh — identity / look / voice (Hale's reader; background only) · L1679–1682]**

**Look.** Lanky, prematurely gray at the temples, wire glasses, a stained Carhartt jacket, a bike he rides everywhere
because he sold his car in the summer (🔒 he couldn't drive past the Point). Smokes American Spirits.
**Voice.** Minnesota-flat, dry, exact; Kierkegaard quotes; long silences. *"Purity of heart is to will one thing. I've
never managed two."*


**[SRC bible.md · §10.10 Caleb — voice (Hale's acolyte; one of twelve at the table) · L1877–1878]**

**Voice.** Central Valley twang, rapid, King James cadence used ironically; self-deprecating; "dude" in moments of terror.
- *"He wrote you back? I wrote him four letters. Four. I used a thesaurus on the third one."*


**[SRC bible.md · §10.11 Yusuf — voice · L1893–1899]**

**Voice.** Bay Area understatement; deadpan; "inshallah" only when he means it; engineering metaphors ("load-bearing,"
"factor of safety"); a running bit about Catholic potlucks.
**(E14 plan:)** Thursday, Fort Hood (the shooter's name and faith on every screen by evening): Jack texts him (Tariq, Helmand) — *Ask me tomorrow.* Friday
at Hale's, quiet, with his mother's dates: "My brother called this morning. He's fine. He said don't watch the news. I watched the news." When a guest
asks what Muslims think, Hale answers for the room ("He's a guest"); Yusuf, after: "Thank you, Professor. I'd like one week where nobody asks." Never a
token; his own joke before the pudding (a Catholic potluck has never once run out of food; "it's the only miracle I've personally verified").
- *"I'm fasting, Professor. My soul is the only part of me that isn't hungry."*


**[SRC bible.md · §12 Doug Pruitt · L1967–1969]**

- **Doug Pruitt** (b. Aug 3, 1991), Kevin's roommate, Bakersfield, mechanical engineering, World of Warcraft (a level-80
  Tauren druid), sweet, oblivious, says "dude" with five vowels. 🔒 He is the 3 a.m. fire-alarm puller — a sleepwalker.
  Revealed S1E20 (planned): **Tue Dec 8, 3:58 a.m., the east-stair pull station on four** — Jack, home from Burbank, catches his wrist ("Dude. The raid boss is in the *elevator*.") and walks him back to 420;


**[SRC bible.md · §12 Gloria Mendes · L2000–2002]**

- **Gloria Mendes**, 63, night waitress at the Anchor diner for 30 years. Rates customers like pies (Jack: "Lattice-top.
  Pretty on top. Everything's leaking underneath."). Hires Jack as night dishwasher (S1E02: "Four out of ten? Hon, you're
  hired."): Tue/Thu/Sat, 7 p.m.–1 a.m., $8.00/hr (California minimum, 2009) plus a share of tips.


**[SRC bible.md · §10.12 Rudy — Voice (heard only: the kitchen radio) · L1911–1912]**

**Voice.** Soft, unhurried, Westside Chicano; "mijo"; never raises it.
- *"First time I washed dishes was at Soledad. Six years. Best job in the building — it's warm, and nobody bothers the dish man."*


*[Packer's note] The Newman Center cast (§11, bible L1924–1964: Mrs. Fitch, the Veritas missionaries, Peter/Kenzie, Gus) is off-page in S2E03 ('the Newman crowd' have seen the status); look it up there if needed.*


# 4. HIDDEN CANON THAT CONSTRAINS THIS EPISODE (🔒)


*[Packer's note] Writers' room only. Everything below constrains what may be PLANTED; none of it may be surfaced (journal / cue / key / previously / logline / nextTime / choice text / narration-as-fact). 4.1 is the outline's own binding lists; 4.2 the dossier private-truth/arc paragraphs for everyone on the page; 4.3 the bible's hidden architecture (secrets register, elision rule, Jack's and Lena's secrets, the Point, hidden knowledge ceilings, the S2 seed plan). The Costigan 🔒 material (Frank knows; Maria doesn't; Danny's plea) is inside §10.8 in Section 3.4; Celeste's Memorial secret is inside §10.9 in Section 3.5; Skye's picture/heat specs are in 1C.*


## 4.1 The outline's binding hidden-canon lists (outlines/s2e03.md rev. 2, verbatim)


**[SRC outlines/s2e03.md · §0 — 'Played canon this episode must honor' and 'The room may re-cut scenes, but may not:' · L103–131]**

**Played canon this episode must honor (no gates):** S2E01 and earlier flat (§9.1); every-path S2E02 canon (`continuity.md` S2E02): Frank went in Dec 27 ("Costigan, Francis." — "Where do I stand?" — "You sit, Frank."); "Third's Ma's Sunday… Don't
let him sit in the truck."; the coffee with cream; "Three straight."; **Frank knows the Saints girl sent the coffee — Maria was told "A friend"**; the 1964 program in a freezer bag; Nonna sleeps in the front-room chair since the fall; Nonna's
rosary *per Cassidy*; **the call** (Tue Dec 29, the basement, the furnace, the battery at 3:41); Lena's market call (home field; the gumbo for "the musician"; "Julian lands tomorrow at four"; "I won't call as much… I'll call."; **she knows
Danny asked Jack not to let Pop sit in the truck, and Jack promised to tell her how it goes**); rung 7 in Frank's hand; Lena's dawn call (Julian peeled shrimp; asleep in Remy's room with his shoes lined up); Rosie's midnight; Ryan's;
Carly's question (🔒); **12:00 Jack answered Skye first, on purpose**; **1:00 Lena's text in the same second**; Skye's den chat ("this is the first one i wanted to be somewhere"; "8 days"; she has his flight on her hand); Laney's wall
(*the ninth*); Skye bought *Orthodoxy* on Ventura; Skye knows Frank said "good."

**The room may re-cut scenes, but may not:**
- let **Lena** learn or guess anything of Skye before the status (Maria, Rosie and Gina on the line say nothing of Skye; Nonna says "*La suora?*" and nothing else within earshot of a live phone — never *l'attrice*, never *Cassidy*);
  say Julian's name between s01 and s09 (the hint is the first in a week); explain the hint, or follow it with anything that settles its meaning ("I wanted you to know first," "before I do it," "you know why" — all cut);
  ask where, who or what anywhere but "How's California?"; raise the status, the photos or Skye on Monday (the proxy-fight grievance is S2E09's, verbatim); be anything but perfect and civil in s18 (no cut, no dig — "Same as
  everybody's" is a fact about a box); borrow Skye's moves or phone-perception (#64, #144); name the withdrawal in the porch's terms;
- let **Jack** tell Lena about Skye by any route; say "I love you" or any declaration to her (the [Unguarded] *I do* inside a rehearsal is the ceiling — never named by anyone); ask Lena what she meant on Friday or what's wrong on Monday;
  reason his way to the right reading; look at the top shelf of the closet in 412 (the duffel is S2E04's); quote the call (his confession wording is his, short, in the box only);
- let **Skye** hear Lena's, Julian's or Kevin's names from Jack (at SBA the dinner is "Ted's thing"; at Buellton he walks out of earshot; in the Fishbowl Doug says "Lena" — **Skye hears a name said by somebody else, once, and lets
  it go by** — her face given one action, no gloss; 🔒 she files it — she clocked "a friend of mine" in italics in November); learn what's in the folder; meet Laney or Lena (S2E04);
  let **Debbie** say her daughter's real name (🔒 S2E09's — "Skye" and "baby" only); let **Corinne's Google** surface anyone on Jack's wall but Jack (no Lena, no Becca, no Gina's wall) — his name, a *Vindicator* box score, the dean's list,
  Theo as a mutual friend, and the folder;
- let **Laney** see Skye and Jack together, or say any part of "a California you" (S2E04's, verbatim); let the alliance **acknowledge** itself aloud (the index card is evidence; the binder stays unread);
- let **Danny** repeat, gloss or allude to his Dec 20 question; finish "And tell the veil girl —"; give advice about either girl; touch the fight, the plea, Mikey, Struthers (§15); write anything an officer wouldn't clear or Maria shouldn't read;
- let the narration **read Frank** (the engine; the Canada Dry — "Your mother likes the bubbles." stands unglossed), **recap a decision** (#90 — especially s09→s09a→s18: the misreading is shown once, never re-explained), quote the call,
  Fr. Dom's "two thousand miles," or Osei's "distance is a mercy";
- let **anyone but Jack** see a picture (at SBA she watches his face); describe any earlier picture again; let a picture into a card, key, recap or Previously;
- spend **S2E04+**: the duffel; Haiti; Laney's first sighting; emergency meeting #2; the overheard fragments; Theo's reserved line ("Never ask her if she's acting." — S2E05); Osei bracing (S2E05); her real name (S2E09); the gossip item and the
  Danny problem proper (S2E08); her reckless posting (S2E05+); the proxy fights (S2E09+); "Laney corners Jack" (S2E06);
- re-run S2E01/S2E02 beats or lines ("Make him."; "They're resting."; "I said I'd call."; "Third's Ma's Sunday" except in the Previously; "Course it is."; "Rich one don't lie to Ma."; "You knew before me."; "He misses the beach"; "Two-eighty-six";
  "Three straight"; the hook-and-eye; the PIT escalator; the bread basket; the levee; the glider; "the NICE one"; the five-day except as Skye's s01 bit; the furnace's flank; the seventh stair more than once; the side door's bang more than once;
  "The line clicks over to nothing"; "Every name on it is true. It's one short.");
- use a typing indicator, read receipt, iMessage, front camera, "selfie," "Facebook official," any app beyond Facebook for iPhone [V], or a comment "Like" (June 2010); quote any lyric; quote Percy or Greene beyond a title and a fact.

## 4.2 Dossier hidden paragraphs (private truth · wound · want · romantic history · alliance · arc) for everyone on the page


### Jack


**[SRC bible.md · §6 Jack — private truth / wound / want / romantic history / arc · L248–252]**

**Private truth. 🔒** He believes he is not a good man and that everyone who loves him is being conned. He has a reason.
(See §17, Jack's secret.) His charm is a way of paying in advance. His faith is real — not inherited, *chosen*, at a
specific moment in a specific place (a holding cell bench, 🔒 §17) — and it is the one thing he's never performed. He
came to Marisol to fight Adrian Hale because Hale's book nearly took that faith from him at sixteen, and because a part
of him wants to lose.


**[SRC bible.md · §6 Jack — private truth / wound / want / romantic history / arc · L254–254]**

**Wound.** 🔒 His brother is in prison for something Jack did.


**[SRC bible.md · §6 Jack — private truth / wound / want / romantic history / arc · L256–257]**

**Want vs. need.** *Want:* to be worth what it cost. *Need:* to accept that he can't be, and that grace is not a payment
plan.


**[SRC bible.md · §6 Jack — private truth / wound / want / romantic history / arc · L282–284]**

**Romantic history (hard rule).** 🔒 Has kissed girls — a CYO dance at fourteen; Angela Russo from Ursuline for most of
junior year, which ended in the winter after November 2007 when he went "quiet" and she couldn't find the door — and has
never slept with anyone. He is not naive and not scandalized; he is waiting on purpose and it is not easy.


**[SRC bible.md · §6 Jack — Arc (head line + S2 tail from L297) · L286–317]**

**Arc (full run; re-planned after S1E11 for the player's MAJOR RE-PLAN, exact sequence and long-range timeline).** From
[… earlier (S1) history omitted by the Packer: bible.md L287–L296 …]
agree to be dating when he gets back (S1E22). **Winter break (S2E01–E02):** a Youngstown Christmas; Skye on the RAZR and the family
Dell; the Costigans find her Facebook. **S2 (Spring 2010):** Skye,
the pull of the world, from January (the darkroom near-miss); the proxy fights; New Orleans — Super Bowl XLIV through
Mardi Gras — breaks it open; both free within a week; he asks, she says no with a straight face, cracks up, and makes him
do it properly; three Lenten dates; **official at the Easter Vigil (Apr 3, 2010)**; the alliance reveal (S2E17); Theo's
truth on the wall, and Danny's, told back (S2E22). **SU1:** Youngstown, long distance; New Orleans for the Fourth as her
boyfriend. **S3 (Fall 2010):** a couple; restraint becomes a thing they have to choose out loud; holding Theo's secret from
the woman he loves while asking everyone else for truth; she tells him about the test (S3E18) and he can't tell her what he
knows. **S4 (Spring 2011):** the best and worst season; Nonna; Theo surrenders (Apr 26, 2011); Lena learns Jack knew for a
year — the rupture. **SU2:** apart; a real come-and-see weekend at the Pontifical College Josephinum in Columbus (the
priesthood flirtation — once, and real); Lena comes to Youngstown in July anyway (brief: they trade visits both summers) and the repair begins; he goes to New Orleans in late August and
Wes dies (Aug 29, 2011) while he is there. **S5 (Fall
2011):** the funeral; repair, explicit about forgiveness; the truth about Danny told to Lena (at the latest here — she must
know whom she's marrying); restraint at its height; **the proposal (S5E22, Gaudete week 2011)**. **S6 (Spring 2012):**
engaged; Pre-Cana; wedding planning; the NFP plan; they choose Ephesians 5 for the wedding (S6E09) — the argument itself was S4's "conversation"
(S4E17, re-planned after S1E13). **SU3:** **the wedding (Sat July 21, 2012, Our Lady of the Holy
Rosary, New Orleans)**. **S7 (Fall 2012):** married, living together in Las Olas; the election; the truth told to Donna
Kostelnik and then the prosecutor, with his wife beside him. **S8 (Spring 2013):** Benedict resigns (it lands hard), Francis
arrives; Danny home; graduation. Adulthood: 🔒 New Orleans first (her law school and the public defender's office; Jack
teaching theology at a Catholic high school there), then home: Cardinal Mooney, and later a prison chaplaincy volunteer at
Trumbull Correctional. He never becomes a priest; the show flirts with it once (SU2) and the flirtation is real.


### Lena


**[SRC bible.md · §7 Lena — private truth / wound / want / romantic history / arc · L333–337]**

**Private truth. 🔒** She is holding three secrets so tightly she has forgotten what her hands feel like open (§18):
what she did in April 2009 at Marisol; what happened to her grandmother at Memorial Medical Center; and how bad things
have quietly become with Wes. Her fierceness is real and it is also armor. She touches her grandmother's Miraculous Medal
when she's thinking — and when she's lying. She doesn't know she does the second. (The player knows the tell — it's in the
brief — but Jack doesn't, and the player never knows *what* she's lying about. Every touch must be honest bookkeeping.)


**[SRC bible.md · §7 Lena — private truth / wound / want / romantic history / arc · L339–340]**

**Wound.** 🔒 Katrina, and the question of what her mother did or didn't do on the fourth day. Also: the dead girl whose
bed she slept beside.


**[SRC bible.md · §7 Lena — private truth / wound / want / romantic history / arc · L347–348]**

**Want vs. need.** *Want:* to be right — to have argued every case so well that nothing can be her fault. *Need:* to be
forgiven for something she can't argue her way out of, and to let someone carry it with her.


**[SRC bible.md · §7 Lena — private truth / wound / want / romantic history / arc · L369–370]**

**Romantic history (hard rule).** 🔒 Wes is her first and only boyfriend (from Feb 2009); before him, a kiss at a Rosary
mixer at sixteen she still rates "a two, generously." She and Wes held the line. She has never slept with anyone.


**[SRC bible.md · §7 Lena — Arc (head line + S2 tail from L379) · L372–390]**

**Arc (full run; re-planned after S1E11).** From advocate to penitent to witness. **S1:** the rival who won't let anyone
[… earlier (S1) history omitted by the Packer: bible.md L373–L378 …]
"When nobody has to be the other anything." **S2:** Jack and Skye — she hates every minute; she does
with Julian exactly what Jack did with Becca and can't end it because she's so angry; the proxy fights; then New Orleans:
the Saints, her family, her city, the honesty; she ends it with Julian herself ("You've never once told me I was wrong"),
says no to Jack with a straight face, cracks up, and makes him do it properly; gives up kissing for Lent "in principle";
her public debate with Hale (S2E13); official at the Easter Vigil. **S3:** a couple; Laney puts the pills down (a night on the
floor of their room, no scare — brief, "Laney: mostly fun"); Thanksgiving with Jack at the Broussards' table; she tells him
about the test (S3E18). **S4:** the confession that costs her and the lie that costs him: when Theo surrenders, her own part
comes out, and she learns Jack knew for a year — the rupture. **SU2/S5:** Wes's death; the hardest grief; forgiveness given
and received; the proposal (S5E22). **S6:** engaged; Pre-Cana; Ephesians 5; the Memorial question finally opened with her
mother. **SU3:** the rehearsal dinner (her mother's secret spoken at last) and the wedding (July 21, 2012 — the eve of her
twenty-first birthday and her saint's day). **S7–S8:** married; the election; Francis, whom she adores; graduation.
Adulthood: 🔒 Tulane Law, then the Orleans Public Defenders — she argues for people no one else will.


### Skye (real name; Debbie; what she knows; her arc to the end)


**[SRC bible.md · §10.2 Skye — name / comes north / physical / wound (Debbie) / on Lena / Laney first sighting / fundraiser / arc · L1225–1231]**

**Name.** **Skye Callender** — "Skye" is the stage name that stuck. 🔒 **Her real name is Mary Catherine Callender-[father's surname]** (brief, binding;
corrected by the Head Writer after S1E14 from the bible's earlier "Jennifer Ann Dahl"; hyphenated per the player's clarification — Callender is her mother
Debbie's surname; the father, a Catholic long gone from her life, is the side that had her baptized and named her for his mother; **the surname is
still the room's to choose before the reveal**): **baptized Catholic, named for a grandmother she barely knew** ("I'm named for a grandmother I met twice," S1E17), which she never uses and nobody at Marisol knows but Theo. The irony isn't lost on Jack. Reveal it late, as an intimacy
(room's call in S2 — the night of the fundraiser walk or the night it ends; never in a recap, never in a journal card before she says it).
**Born April 4, 1990** — 19 when they meet (Nov 2009), 20 in April 2010. Lancaster, in the Antelope Valley, then a two-bedroom in
Studio City her mother rented for the commute.


**[SRC bible.md · §10.2 Skye — name / comes north / physical / wound (Debbie) / on Lena / Laney first sighting / fundraiser / arc · L1242–1245]**

**She comes north early (accelerated after S1E13):** *Sweetwater* wraps its fall block at the end of October; she sees Sol's Halloween
footage in Silver Lake the first week of November and drives up about **Sun Nov 15** to crash on Sol's porch and practice being normal
before anybody knows she's there. **She doesn't tell Theo** ("Ted would throw me a party. With a theme.") and Sol, who talks very
little, doesn't either.


**[SRC bible.md · §10.2 Skye — name / comes north / physical / wound (Debbie) / on Lena / Laney first sighting / fundraiser / arc · L1259–1264]**

**Physical (brief; HARD RULE).** **Jack goes farther with her than with anyone ever** — frank, charged, on the page within the brief's
literary standard — **and never sleeps with her**, and **it drives her crazy**; **at times she legitimately tries to seduce him** (the Del Playa
house; her car above Zuma; Theo's Malibu pool house at Presidents' weekend, room's call). This is the real test of his resolve and restraint:
every such scene costs resolve (−1 to −2) and owes a confession; an exit is always on the menu, and if the player won't take it, she or the
world does. Ceiling: making out, real temptation, the near-miss. **Lena knows or suspects enough to be furious.** Later, with Lena, it becomes
part of Jack's own guilt and honesty (S2E19: "Never. Not with anyone.").


**[SRC bible.md · §10.2 Skye — name / comes north / physical / wound (Debbie) / on Lena / Laney first sighting / fundraiser / arc · L1272–1278]**

**Her wound (brief): the child-star machine.** Working since eleven; a stage mother (**Debbie Callender**, who still signs her emails "Momager");
no childhood; everyone wanting something — a callback, a photo, a favor for a nephew. **Jack is the first person who wants nothing from her
career**, and **his refusal to sleep with her is the first "no" she has ever met that isn't a negotiation.** That is part of why it drives her
crazy, and part of why she falls for him. Never trauma-as-decoration: it comes out in a reflex (she smiles for anyone who raises a phone; she
can't eat in front of a stranger without checking the angle), and once, in the real voice, in a sentence.
**On Lena (brief).** **She clocks it instantly** — knows on day one that Jack is in love with Lena, and decides to win anyway. Gracious to Lena's
face; never once fooled. ("She's the one, isn't she. The one with the medal. Don't answer, Jack, you're a terrible actor.")


**[SRC bible.md · §10.2 Skye — name / comes north / physical / wound (Debbie) / on Lena / Laney first sighting / fundraiser / arc · L1290–1292]**

**Laney's first sighting (brief; the player's line, verbatim).** The first time Laney sees Skye and Jack together — **S2E04, Wed Jan 13, 2010, the Newman
Haiti table, Skye in a borrowed apron at Jack's elbow** (moved from S1E22 after S1E13: the December is secret) — she says to Lena: **"Oh my God, he found a California you. Excuse me,
while I go be a bitch on your behalf."** Then she goes and does it (alliance rules). **Lena's reaction is the knife: she hates that Laney's right.**


**[SRC bible.md · §10.2 Skye — name / comes north / physical / wound (Debbie) / on Lena / Laney first sighting / fundraiser / arc · L1320–1320]**

**Arc (timing ACCELERATED after S1E13 — player directive, brief "ACCELERATED SEQUENCE"; design per the player).**

Asking."** at the height — his no (`e22_tue`) — **heat E22a** (above). **Thu 12:50 a.m., the bluff above Ellery, the new moon:** she coasts the bluff road with the headlights off; **"Thursday.   I'd be better than her at something. Explain to me, slowly, why it took you a parade."** She is not cruel and not fooled; she kisses his cheek like a
